<note>This manuscript contains an astronomical/astrological table, likely a "Lagnapatrasadhani" used to calculate the ascendant (lagna). The abbreviations 'मे', 'वृ', 'मि', 'क', 'सि', 'क' correspond to the zodiac signs Mesha (Aries) through Kanya (Virgo). The columns (0-27) likely refer to divisions of the zodiac, possibly Nakshatras. Numerical values represent units of time or angular distance (degrees, minutes, seconds).</note>

<vocab>श्रीगणेशायनमः, रापचरखंडानि, लमपत्रसाधनी, रेखा, मे (Mesha), वृ (Vrishabha), मि (Mithuna), क (Karka), सि (Simha), क (Kanya)</vocab>

| १ | ३ | २ | २ | ५ | ४ | ७ | १७ | २७ |
| ९ | २४ | २४ | २ | ३२ | ५ | २६ | २५ | ५२ |
| ४२ | ४९ | ४५ | ५० | ३२ | ३७ | २७ | २ | ५२ |
| ११ | ११ | ० | ० | ० | ० | ० | ० | ० |

---

अथां क ने चक्र मुनि ते रू तेय
ति य न्त्रा घ. पल ने ६० ना गुणेर
ये अं श ने ३० भाग ले इ येते ता
शि मा स् क ये र्द्वाद श उ परा नं
रो य ती २ जाग ले इ ये गो प रे ह
ते ने पै ह ए क सां धि ही न क रि ये
या रे च क्रा का ना को ट क था या ॥

---

<meta>The manuscript page is an astronomical table or ephemeris (Sayan Lagna Patram) calculated for Udaipur. It uses Devanagari script for both text and numerals. The table lists values for various zodiac signs (Rashis) across 31 degrees/days.</meta>

<margin>१०</margin>

> ॥ इति श्रीभौमकोष्टकाएकोनाशीतिसंरूपा॥ छ॥ ॥ श्रीवरदमूर्तिर्जयति॥ १॥

<lang>san</lang>

<meta>The manuscript page contains an astronomical table (ephemeris) for the planet Mercury, titled "Budhaḥ" (बुध:). The data is organized in three main horizontal blocks, each corresponding to a four-year cycle. The table tracks planetary positions and phenomena across 27 columns, likely representing the Nakshatras (lunar mansions). The script is Devanagari, and the grid is hand-drawn in red ink.</meta>

The top section is dedicated to combined calculations for Saturn and Mercury (Shani-Budha). The bottom section is titled "Madhyamah" (Mean Position) and includes year references in the margin (likely Shaka or Samvat era years).

<meta>The manuscript is an astronomical ephemeris for the planet Mercury (Budha), likely from the 16th or 17th century. It features three horizontal data blocks, each corresponding to a set of years (possibly in Vikram Samvat) noted in the left margin. The table has 27 columns representing the 27 Nakshatras (lunar mansions). Text is in Sanskrit written in Devanagari script. Red ink is used for the grid lines.</meta>

<meta>This page contains an astronomical table for the planet Jupiter (Guru), organized by the 27 Nakshatras. The values likely represent planetary positions or mean motions over specific time periods. The years listed in the right column (1841-1844 AD and corresponding Shaka/Samvat years 1554-1557) suggest the table's temporal range.</meta>

<vocab>गुरु (Jupiter), धनं (Addition/Plus), अग्रसार (Ephemeris/Advancement), कोष्टक (Table), शा (Shaka era abbreviation)</vocab>

<meta>Astronomical table for Saturn (Shani), likely an ephemeris for calculating planetary longitudes. The vertical columns (1-27) likely correspond to lunar mansions (Nakshatras) or specific days, while the rows represent cycles across years marked in the margin (198-201, possibly Samvat years).</meta>

<note>The table shows calculated increments for the motion of Saturn (Shani). The upper section (marked १) provides the daily motion for 1 to 25 days, calculated at a rate of 2 minutes and 1 second per day. The lower section (marked २) provides cumulative motion for intervals of 25 days (e.g., Column 1 corresponds to 25 days, Column 2 to 50 days, etc.), using an increment of 50 minutes and 25 seconds.</note>

म ४ म ३ न ५ न ६ न ७ न ८

<note>This manuscript page contains an astronomical/astrological table for Shani (the planet Saturn), as indicated by the title at the top. The table is structured into 27 columns corresponding to the Nakshatras (lunar mansions) of the Hindu zodiac. The numerical values likely represent angular measurements (degrees and minutes) or temporal calculations associated with the planet's movement through these divisions. The right-hand column and marginal notes use "म" (Ma), "न" (Na), and numerals, which are typical shorthand in Jyotisha (Vedic astrology) for categorical classifications or strengths.</note>

<vocab>शनि (Shani), Saturn, Jyotisha, Nakshatra, Devanagari numerals, Sanskrit, ephemeris</vocab>

**मं बु बृ शु श रा**
